I 258 CUSTOMS GAZETTE. [APRIL-JUNE,. (Received 20th July 1905.) CUSTOM HOUSE, KOWLOON, 12th July 1905. SIR, I.&mdash;a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 381, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Kowloon and the neighbouring stations in Native vessels for the second quarter of the year 1905. b: The stations at present controlled by the Kowloon Customs are Taishan (A &sect;1), Lintin (ffr ft)&#39; and Shamchun (&sect;? iJU), on the north and west; Samun ( &mdash; PI), on the east; and Shaiichung and Shatowkok gjj ), on the Mirs Bay littoral. c. The word &quot;Inwards&quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot;Outwards,&quot; from China. II. 066 CUSTOM8 GAZETT8. [APRIL-J-UNE, (Beosv#d 5th uly g.) CUSTOM HOUSE, LAPPA, IITH JULYr 19go. SIR$ I.-a.
